Part A verdict: the complete frozen first-order object survives the independent
original-variable exact readback. All63 coefficients, all36 strict sign prices,
all14 zero box prices, the svec convention, and the full dual LDL product pass.

Part B verdict at DRAFT level: the full zero-capacity tangent consists only of
the zero direction. This yields a genuine neighborhood theorem for EVERY full
original certificate, with a strict linear capacity loss and a uniform bound
on coefficient displacement. The proof rederives base uniqueness and local
boundedness jointly and controls the complete nonlinear remainder. Neither a
global seven-step capacity ceiling nor the original minimax problem is settled.

## 1. Objects, accepted input, and the proposed theorem

All vectors and matrices below are real unless exact field data are specified.
Write q58=sqrt(2), lambda=1+q58, u=q58-1=1/lambda, and

    hbar=(q58,2,q58,1+lambda,q58,2,q58),  Abar=lambda^3.

For h=(h0,...,h6) in [0,infinity)^7, let s=sum_i h_i and A(h)=1+s.
Let e be the all-ones column in R8, and let e_i be its coordinate columns,
indexed0,...,7. For any zero-diagonal real8-by8 array X define

    L(X)=diag(Xe)-X.

The prefix matrix T(h) has diagonal1, entry h_j at i,j when j<i, and zero
above its diagonal. Put

    omega(h)=(-2h0,...,-2h6,2s)^T,
    D(h)=diag(h0(2-h0),...,h6(2-h6),-s(s+2)),
    M(h,Lambda)=D(h)+sym(L(Lambda)T(h)),
    sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2.

A full certificate means precisely

    Lambda_ii=0, Lambda_ij>=0 for ALL ordered i!=j,
    L(Lambda)^T e=omega(h), M(h,Lambda)>=0 on the ENTIRE R8.       (1)

All56 ordered coefficients and all8 independent formal gradients remain.
No support, symmetry, rank, or equality between formal gradients is imposed
in(1). Nonzero singular PSD remainders are included.

Accepted fact04219554e41d9750, proof Sections10--11, supplies the original
primitive coefficient identity and the full original-coordinate composition
identity. For a normalized convex1-smooth objective F, write g_i=grad F(x_i)
and f_i=F(x_i); Gram(g0,...,g7) has entries <g_i,g_j>. Its Section10 uses

    x_i=x0-sum_(k<i) h_k g_k,
    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
    V_h=2sum_(i<7)h_i(f_i-f7)
          +sum_(i<7)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g7||^2,

and establishes, under the flow in(1),

    V_h+sum_(i!=j) Lambda_ij Q_ij
       =trace(M(h,Lambda) Gram(g0,...,g7)).                    (2)

This accepted fact is the only non-elementary accepted input used here.
Its original all-function interpretation is retained; no converse about all
primitive schedules is asserted. The exact algebraic statement of its join
identity needed below is recalled in Section5.

Define Lambdabar by the following twenty entries, with every other entry0:

    01:2                    10:2lambda                13:2lambda
    21:2lambda*q58            23:2lambda^2+2lambda*q58    31:2lambda
    32:2lambda^3            37:2lambda^2              43:2lambda*q58
    45:2lambda^2            47:2lambda*q58              53:4lambda
    54:2lambda^3            57:2lambda^3+4lambda      63:2lambda*q58
    65:2lambda^3*q58          67:2lambda^4+2lambda^3*q58+2lambda*q58
    73:2lambda              75:2lambda^3              76:2lambda^5.

These are positive. The independently rebuilt original flow is exact and
M(hbar,Lambdabar)=0. A second algebraic justification is to join two singleton
q58 words at bridge2 and join two resulting three-step words at bridge1+lambda,
using the accepted composition identity; each right scaling is lambda^2 and
each extra interpolation factor is lambda^2-1=2lambda.

PROPOSED LOCAL THEOREM. There exist epsilon>0 and c>0 such that for EVERY
h in[0,infinity)^7 and EVERY array Lambda satisfying(1), if
||h-hbar||_2<epsilon, then

    A(h) <= Abar-c*sqrt(||h-hbar||_2^2+||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F^2). (3)

Here ||.||_F is the Frobenius matrix norm. In particular A(h)<=lambda^3;
equality requires h=hbar and Lambda=Lambdabar. Moreover each such array obeys

    ||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F <= (sqrt(7)/c)*||h-hbar||_2.          (4)

This is a bound relative to the base for all selections, not an assertion of
uniqueness of certificates away from the base or of a Lipschitz selection map
between arbitrary pairs of nearby words. A precise positive definition of c
is given in Section8. The proof establishes existence of epsilon; no numerical
radius is asserted.

## 2. Complete linearization, common null, and exact dual identity

Let E58 be the twenty ordered pairs listed above and Z0 the other36 ordered
distinct pairs. Write Tbal58=T(hbar) and Lbar=L(Lambdabar). Tbal58 is exactly the frozen candidate's prefix matrix T0. The separate name
avoids collision with the benchmark recurrence map called T0 in the glossary.
For p in R7 and a zero-diagonal Y in R^(8-by-8), put

    S=sum_(i=0)^6 p_i,
    Tp_ij=p_j if j<i, and0 otherwise,
    op=(-2p0,...,-2p6,2S)^T,
    D1(p)=diag(2(1-hbar_i)p_i for i<7,-2Abar*S),
    W(p,Y)=D1(p)+sym(Lbar*Tp+L(Y)*Tbal58),
    f(p,Y)=L(Y)^T e-op, n(p,Y)=W(p,Y)e.                     (5)

The FULL linearized cone is

    C58={(p,Y): f(p,Y)=0, Y_ij>=0 on Z0, W(p,Y)>=0 on R8}.    (6)

The twenty Y entries on E58 are free. No p-box occurs in(6).

For any h and any real Lambda with flow, L(Lambda)e=0 and

    omega(h)^T T(h)e=s^2+sum_i h_i^2,
    e^T D(h)e=-s^2-sum_i h_i^2,

so e^T M(h,Lambda)e=0. To check the first equality directly, the entries of
T(h)e are1+sum_(j<i)h_j; expanding the product uses
2sum_(j<i)h_i h_j=s^2-sum_i h_i^2. Differentiation along the exactly flowing
linear families in(5) gives e^T W e=0 when f=0. If W is PSD, expansion of
(e+t z)^T W(e+t z) for arbitrary real t and z forces z^T W e=0, hence n=0.

Let V be the8-by7 matrix whose columns are e_i-e_7 for i<7. Its columns span
e-perp. Put B=V^T W V and G=V^T V=I7+11^T, where1 is the all-ones column
of length7. When We=0, every vector is a multiple of e plus a vector Vz;
therefore W>=0 on R8 if and only if B>=0 on R7. The same decomposition shows
W>=m(I8-ee^T/8) if and only if B>=mG for any real m. Thus the common-null
congruence preserves the original PSD condition without discarding a gradient.

Appendix A contains the complete exact field data for vectors alpha,beta in
Q(q58)^8, a symmetric Z in Q(q58)^(7-by-7), and prices sigma_ij for ij in Z0.
Alpha and beta are respectively the first and last eight equality_prices.
The 28 PSD_svec values are in upper-triangular column-major order. A diagonal
entry is Z_ii; an off-diagonal entry is q58*Z_ij. The precise identity is

    -S = trace(ZB)+sum_(ij in Z0)sigma_ij Y_ij
                  -alpha^T f-beta^T n.                     (7)

It holds for EVERY p and EVERY real zero-diagonal Y, before imposing(6).
All14 box prices are exactly0. Consequently(7) is homogeneous and applies
to arbitrary p; no normalization from the discovery optimization survives.

Here are also the63 coefficient equations in direct algebraic form. Let
H=VZV^T, K=H-sym(beta e^T), let t_i be the transpose of row i of Tbal58, and
let r_ij=t_i-t_j. The unit Y_ij contribution is

    f=e_i-e_j, W=sym(e_i r_ij^T).

Thus the56 equations are

    alpha_i-alpha_j-(K r_ij)_i=0       for ij in E58,
    alpha_i-alpha_j-(K r_ij)_i=sigma_ij for ij in Z0.          (8)

For each i<7 let C_i have entry1 in column i strictly below its diagonal,
and all other entries0. The unit p_i contribution is

    N_i=2(1-hbar_i)e_i e_i^T-2Abar e_7e_7^T+sym(Lbar C_i),
    f=2e_i-2e_7.

The other seven equations are

    2(alpha_i-alpha_7)-trace(K N_i)=1, i=0,...,6.             (9)

The independent executable in Appendix B did NOT use(8)--(9) as a shortcut.
It rebuilt(5) for each of the63 unit inputs from the literal original hbar,
Lambdabar, prefix matrix and row-sum Laplacian. For every input it checked(7),
trace(ZB)=trace(HW), the q58-weighted svec inner product, and

    e^T W e=f^T Tbal58 e.

Its complete result retains all original f,W,We,B arrays. Every coefficient
residual is the rational pair(0,0). Source, input and expected-check hashes
were frozen before the sole charged job. No supplied passed flag or supplied
stationarity residual was used; no author checker was imported.

Field arithmetic uses pairs(r,s) representing r+s*q58, with
(r,s)*(a,b)=(ra+2sb,rb+sa). Inversion multiplies by the conjugate and divides
by r^2-2s^2. Every denominator check is exact. For signs, if r,s have the
same nonzero sign use that sign; if they have opposite signs use
sign(r)*sign(r^2-2s^2). Zero-coordinate cases are immediate. Appendix A's
36 sigma values all have sign+1 by this exact rule.

Appendix A also gives Z=R diag(d0,...,d6) R^T. The independent readback checks
that R is unit lower triangular, every one of the49 product entries is Z's
corresponding entry, and all seven d_i have exact sign+1. Thus Z is positive
definite. No floating eigenvalue is a premise. Let r_k be column k of R, a
vector of length7. Since

    trace(ZB)=sum_k d_k r_k^T B r_k,

this trace is nonnegative for PSD B. If it vanishes, each of the seven
nonnegative quadratic forms vanishes; the PSD argument used for the common
null then makes B annihilate the basis formed by the columns of R. Hence B=0.

For(6), f=n=0, B>=0, and every active Y is nonnegative. Identity(7) proves
S<=0. If S=0, strict positivity of every sigma and positive definiteness of Z
give

    Y_ij=0 for every ij in Z0, B=0, W=0.                    (10)

Up to this point a nonzero equality direction has NOT been excluded. The
next two sections establish its exclusion by original-coordinate algebra.

## 3. A three-step zero-remainder derivative calculation

Use local indices0,1,2,3 and the three-step base word(q58,2,q58), of capacity
lambda^2. In this section L,T,omega,D,M are defined exactly as in Section1
with three steps and four gradients, so the terminal index is3 and e is the
all-ones column of R4. Its base array has seven positive entries

    01=2, 10=13=31=2lambda, 21=2lambda*q58,
    23=2lambda^2+2lambda*q58, 32=2lambda^3,                    (11)

and all other entries0. Its remainder is0 by the accepted singleton join.
Permit derivatives on exactly these seven pairs AND the additional pair03.
The latter derivative is an unrestricted real number. Write r0,r1,r2 for
the physical derivatives, y_ij for the coefficient derivatives, and assume
the four original flow derivatives hold and the full4-by4 remainder
derivative Wloc is0. We prove all r and y are0.

Only the pairs in(11) and03 are used in the following displayed entries.
The entry02 of L(Lambda)T is -h2*Lambda03, whereas its entry20 is0.
Therefore

    (Wloc)_02=-q58*y03/2,

which forces y03=0. The flow at0 and the entries00 and01 now give

    y01-y10=-2r0,
    -2q*r0-u*y01=0,
    -y01+2lambda*r0+u*y10=0.                               (12)

The first two equations give y10=y01+2r0 and y01=-2q*lambda*r0.
Substitution in the last equation yields

    [-2q*lambda*(u-1)+4q]r0=4lambda*r0=0.

Thus r0=y01=y10=0. Entries11 and13 and flow at1 give, in order,

    y13=-2(1+lambda)r1,
    y31=y13-2lambda*r1,
    y21=2(1+lambda)r1.                                     (13)

For clarity, these equations come from

    (Wloc)_11=-2(1+lambda)r1-y13,
    2(Wloc)_13=-y13+y31+2lambda*r1,
    y13-y21-y31=-2r1.

The12 entry, obtained from -h2*Lambda13+(h1-1)*Lambda21, gives

    0=-q58*y13-2lambda*r2+y21+2lambda*q58*r1
      =4lambda^2*r1-2lambda*r2.

Consequently r2=2lambda*r1.

A scalar identity supplies the last physical equation without eliminating
any formal gradient. For a word h of length3 let v=T(h)^(-1)e. Its entries
satisfy v0=1 and v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i. Since L(Lambda)e=0,

    v^T M(h,Lambda)v=v^T D(h)v=1-A(h)^2*v3^2.               (14)

The second equality follows from
h_i(2-h_i)v_i^2=v_i^2-v_(i+1)^2 and -s(s+2)=1-A^2.
At the base, M=0, A=lambda^2 and v3=-u^2. Differentiating(14) therefore
gives the necessary equality

    (r0+r1+r2)/lambda^2+lambda*(r0+r2)+r1=0.               (15)

The moving-vector derivative contributes0 because the base matrix is0.
Substituting r0=0 and r2=2lambda*r1 into(15) gives

    [(1+2lambda)/lambda^2+2lambda^2+1]r1=0.

The bracket is strictly positive, so r1=r2=0. Equations(13) give
y13=y31=y21=0. Entry22 now reads y21-u*y23=0, so y23=0; flow at2 gives
y21+y23-y32=0, so y32=0. Together with(12) and y03=0, this proves that
the full derivative is zero. No sign assumption on these eight derivatives
was needed. This additional03 entry is retained because it arises when the
right child of the seven-step array is extracted.

## 4. Exact central-cut algebra to first order

Assume p,Y satisfy f=0, W=0, and Y=0 on Z0. Thus all coefficient derivatives
are supported on E58. Introduce the REAL, not assumed feasible, families

    h(t)=hbar+t*p, Lambda(t)=Lambdabar+t*Y.

Their flow holds exactly for every real t. Their full remainder is O(t^2),
entry by entry, because its constant and linear coefficients are0. Here
O(t^2) means a function with absolute value bounded by a fixed multiple of
t^2 for sufficiently small |t|; the constants may depend on the fixed p,Y.
All computations in this section retain constant and first-order terms.

Let P58={0,1,2,3}, Bset={4,5,6,7}, and let eP,eB be their indicator columns
in R8. Define capacities and bridge excess from these ACTUAL physical steps:

    a(t)=1+h0+h1+h2, b(t)=1+h4+h5+h6, d(t)=h3-1.

Write h_left=(h0,h1,h2) and h_right=(h4,h5,h6). At t=0, a=b=lambda^2 and
d=lambda. The only P58-to-Bset coefficient is
N(t)=Lambda37. The only Bset-to-P58 coefficients are mu_j(t)=Lambda_(j,3)
for j=4,5,6,7. This support follows from(10) and the displayed E58, rather
than from an ansatz about general feasible certificates.

Let LambdaL be the P58-by-P58 subarray, interpreted as a three-step certificate
array for h0,h1,h2. Its original flow holds exactly: the first three rows
have no crossing edge, and the terminal flow follows by summing all four
row-minus-column sums. Direct matrix multiplication gives

    M(h,Lambda)_(P58,P58)
      =M(h_left,LambdaL)+(a^2-d^2-d*N)diag(0,0,0,1).        (16)

Indeed the only crossing contribution within this principal block is
N*(T_3-T_7) in row3; it changes only entry33, by -d*N. The parent-versus-child
D difference at that entry is (1-d^2)-(1-a^2)=a^2-d^2. The other entries
agree. Thus every derivative entry of M(h_left,LambdaL) except possibly33
is0. The scalar flow identity e^T M e=0 from Section2 also holds for this
child at every t, forcing the remaining derivative entry to vanish. Hence

    M(h_left,LambdaL)=O(t^2), a^2-d^2-d*N=O(t^2).            (17)

For j in Bset, direct multiplication by eP yields

    2(M eP)_j=d*mu_j-N*h_j, j=4,5,6,
    2(M eP)_7=d*mu_7-N.                                  (18)

To see every contribution in(18), T eP equals a+d on Bset and a at index3,
so (L T eP)_j=d*mu_j. On Bset, L^T eP has only the entry -N at7.
Upper triangular multiplication by T^T therefore contributes -N*h_j
for j<7 and -N at7. D eP vanishes on Bset. This proves(18) without
any positivity or remainder-rank hypothesis.

Since M=O(t^2) and d stays positive, (18) gives

    mu_j=(N/d)h_j+O(t^2), j=4,5,6;
    mu_7=N/d+O(t^2); sum_j mu_j=N*b/d+O(t^2).

Summing the exact original flow over P58 gives

    sum_j mu_j-N=2(a+d).

It follows that

    N(b-d)=2d(a+d)+O(t^2).                                (19)

Multiplying(19) by d and using d*N=a^2-d^2+O(t^2) from(17), then dividing
by the positive a+d, gives

    (a-d)(b-d)=2d^2+O(t^2),
    a*b-d(a+b)-d^2=O(t^2).                                (20)

This saturated-bridge relation has been DERIVED for the zero-remainder
derivative; it was not imposed on the full tangent cone.

## 5. Extraction of both complete children and the full equality kernel

For positive a,b near lambda^2 define the positive root

    dhat=(-(a+b)+sqrt((a+b)^2+4ab))/2,
    Cjoin=a+b+dhat, kappa=(Cjoin/b)^2.

These functions are twice continuously differentiable for a,b in a sufficiently
small positive neighborhood of their base values, so all derivatives below exist.

The identity a*b=dhat*Cjoin is exact. Equation(20) and the factorization

    a*b-d(a+b)-d^2=-(d-dhat)(a+b+d+dhat)

show d-dhat=O(t^2). Consequently (17)--(19) yield

    N=dhat*(kappa-1)+O(t^2),
    mu_j=(kappa-1)h_j+O(t^2), j=4,5,6,
    mu_7=kappa-1+O(t^2).                                  (21)

For the first equality use N=(a^2-d^2)/d+O(t^2), replace d by dhat, and
use a/dhat=Cjoin/b. All divisions have nonzero positive base values.

Define a real child array on Bset, with local indices0,...,3, by

    LambdaR_ij=Lambda_(i,j)/kappa
       -(1-1/kappa)h_i*1_(i<7,j=7), i,j in Bset, i!=j.     (22)

Keep its diagonal0. No sign assumption on LambdaR is made. Its nonterminal
flow is -2h_j+O(t^2): the RR subarray's flow at j<7 equals -2h_j-mu_j;
after division and subtraction in(22) it is

    (-2h_j-mu_j)/kappa-(1-1/kappa)h_j=-2h_j+O(t^2).

The terminal flow then follows by summing the four row-minus-column sums.

Here is the exact full-space composition identity used to extract remainders.
For our two physical child words of capacities a,b and bridge1+dhat, the
bridge index is3 and the terminal index is7. Let j range over4,5,6, the right
child's nonterminal indices. Accepted fact04219554e41d9750, Section11, gives

    V_parent=V_left+kappa*V_right-(kappa-1)*Ejoin,
    Ejoin=sum_j h_j Q_(j,3)+Q_(7,3)+dhat Q_(3,7)
                                             +sum_j h_j Q_(j,7). (23)

Its coefficient proof uses dhat*(a+b+dhat)=ab and kappa=((a+b+dhat)/b)^2.
Thus the joined multiplier array consists of LambdaL on P58, kappa*LambdaR
on Bset plus (kappa-1)h_i into7 from each nonterminal right index, and
the cross multipliers dhat(kappa-1), (kappa-1)h_j, kappa-1. Under child flow
the FULL remainder is the disjoint original-coordinate block sum
M_left plus kappa*M_right. This is an algebraic coefficient identity; its
validity does not require the child arrays to be nonnegative or their
remainders to be PSD. In the current application child flow holds through
first order, which is precisely the order at which we use this identity.
More explicitly, for an arbitrary real child array with flow defect
xi=L(Lambda)^T e-omega, collecting the same coefficients as in(2) gives

    V+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=trace(M Gram)
                           +sum_i xi_i(f_i-||g_i||^2/2).

The function-value coefficients gain xi_i and the gradient diagonal gains
-xi_i/2. This exact formula shows explicitly that an O(t^2) flow defect
cannot alter the constant or linear matrix coefficients in the join identity.

Replace the bridge by1+dhat and crossings by the exact expressions in(23).
By(21) these changes are O(t^2); the internal arrays are unchanged. Combining
the original identity(2) with(23), or just collecting its gradient coefficients,
therefore gives

    M(h,Lambda)=M(h_left,LambdaL) direct-sum
                   kappa*M(h_right,LambdaR)+O(t^2).        (24)

The flow errors in(22) are O(t^2), so cannot alter a constant or linear
coefficient in this equality. Equations(17) and(24) imply the right child
remainder is also O(t^2). Thus both children have exact base flow, zero
base remainder, correct derivative flow, and zero derivative remainder.

The left child's derivative support is the seven pairs in(11). The right
child's support is those same seven pairs plus03. Explicitly its original
internal allowed pairs are45,47,54,57,65,67,75,76; the subtractions in(22)
only affect47,57,67. Hence no other pair is introduced. At the base,
kappa=lambda^2 and1-1/kappa=2/lambda. Substitution in(22) gives

    LambdaR_01=2, LambdaR_03=0,
    LambdaR_10=LambdaR_13=LambdaR_31=2lambda,
    LambdaR_21=2lambda*q58,
    LambdaR_23=2lambda^2+2lambda*q58,
    LambdaR_32=2lambda^3.

For example its03 entry is (2lambda*q58)/lambda^2-(2/lambda)q58=0;
its13 entry is (2lambda^3+4lambda)/lambda^2-(2/lambda)*2=2lambda;
its23 entry is
(2lambda^4+2lambda^3*q58+2lambda*q58)/lambda^2-(2/lambda)q58
=2lambda^2+2lambda*q58.
The other five listed entries are obtained by division by lambda^2.

Apply Section3 separately to these two complete four-gradient derivatives.
It gives

    p0=p1=p2=p4=p5=p6=0,
    LambdaL'(0)=LambdaR'(0)=0.

Differentiating(20) now leaves

    -(2lambda^2+2lambda)*p3=0,

so p3=0. Then a'=b'=dhat'=kappa'=0. Equations(21) give N'=mu_j'=0,
and differentiating(22) gives all original RR coefficient derivatives0.
The PP and crossing derivatives are already0. Consequently

    f=0, W=0, Y|Z0=0  ==>  p=0 and Y=0.                   (25)

This conclusion was obtained by textual exact algebra only, with no rank
computation, machine elimination or extra mathematical job. Together with(10)
it identifies the FULL cone's zero-capacity equality set:

    (p,Y) in C58 and S=0  iff  (p,Y)=(0,0).                (26)

## 6. Uniqueness at the base, rederived from the same joint identity

Let Lambda be ANY full feasible array at hbar. Put p=0 and
Y=Lambda-Lambdabar. The full flow gives f=0. Because p=0, no nonlinear term
occurs: W(0,Y)=M(hbar,Lambda). On each of the36 zero base entries,
Y_ij=Lambda_ij>=0. Thus (0,Y) belongs to C58 and has S=0. Equation(26)
gives Y=0. We have proved

    Lambda feasible at hbar ==> Lambda=Lambdabar.          (27)

This proof covers every base certificate and every PSD rank. It does not
assume the all-base uniqueness claim of the earlier b937bb05ba1645b1 draft.

## 7. Uniform bounds and convergence for arbitrary nearby certificate choices

Work inside the compact cube h_i in[1,4], which contains hbar in its interior.
Consider the ONE scalar function F(x)=k*x^2/2, with k=1/8, minimizer0 and
x0=1. Its actual GD positions satisfy

    x_i=product_(j<i)(1-h_j/8), 1/2<=1-h_j/8<=7/8.

They strictly decrease and stay positive. If0<=i<j<=7, then

    x_i-x_j >= x_i-x_(i+1)=(h_i/8)x_i >=2^-9,

because i<=6 and x_i>=2^-6. Direct expansion of all ordered residuals gives

    Q_ij=-k(1-k)(x_i-x_j)^2/2 <=-7/2^25 for i!=j.           (28)

This checks every original ordered finite pair for the same actual quadratic.
Its gradients are g_i=k*x_i. In V_h, all terms h_i(1-h_i)g_i^2 and the
terminal negative square are nonpositive, whereas f_i-f7<=f_i<=k/2.
Since sum_i h_i<=28, we have V_h<=28k=7/2. Applying(2) to any feasible
Lambda, using full PSD and all its nonnegative entries, gives

    (7/2^25)sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij
       <=sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij(-Q_ij)<=V_h<=7/2.

Hence EVERY certificate in this cube satisfies

    sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij<=2^24.                             (29)

The bound is intentionally coarse and purely analytic. In particular the
entire set of coefficient arrays is bounded independently of their selection.

Now take any sequence h_k->hbar and choose any feasible Lambda_k. Eventually
the words lie in the cube. A subsequence of the arrays converges by(29).
The limit is nonnegative with zero diagonal, has the exact base flow, and has
a PSD base remainder by continuity: each quadratic form is a limit of
nonnegative quadratic forms. Equation(27) identifies the limit as Lambdabar.
If uniform convergence over all selections failed, one could choose such a
sequence with ||Lambda_k-Lambdabar||_F bounded away from0; its convergent
subsequence would contradict(27). Therefore

    h->hbar through feasible words implies Lambda->Lambdabar
    uniformly over ALL feasible choices of Lambda.         (30)

No estimate on ||Lambda-Lambdabar||/||h-hbar|| was used. Such quotients are
allowed to be unbounded at this stage of the argument.

## 8. Exact nonlinear remainder and strict local capacity loss

For arbitrary finite displacements p=h-hbar, Y=Lambda-Lambdabar, the full
polynomial expansion is EXACT:

    M(h,Lambda)=W(p,Y)+R2(p,Y),
    R2(p,Y)=diag(-p0^2,...,-p6^2,-S^2)+sym(L(Y)Tp).          (31)

Flow is exactly f(p,Y)=0. Every original sign is retained:
Y_ij>=0 on Z0 and Lambdabar_ij+Y_ij>=0 on E58. There are no omitted terms in
(31). In particular the mixed coefficient-step term is retained, and higher
orders arising from a nonlinear choice of h,Lambda are already covered by
this exact displacement formula.

Put ||(p,Y)||_* = sqrt(||p||_2^2+||Y||_F^2). Cauchy--Schwarz gives

    ||L(Y)||_F <=(sqrt(7)+1)||Y||_F,
    ||Tp||_F <=sqrt(7)||p||_2,
    ||diag(-p_i^2,-S^2)||_F <=||p||_2^2+S^2<=8||p||_2^2.

The first bound uses seven off-diagonal entries in each row. The second uses
||Tp||_F^2=sum_(j=0)^6(7-j)p_j^2. Symmetrization does not increase Frobenius
norm, and the Frobenius norm of a product is bounded by the product of norms.
Consequently

    ||R2(p,Y)||_F <=8||p||_2^2+(7+sqrt(7))||p||_2||Y||_F
                   <=13||(p,Y)||_*^2.                     (32)

Define the compact set

    K58={(p,Y) in C58: ||(p,Y)||_*=1}.

It is closed because flow and W are linear and the sign and PSD cones are
closed; it lies on a finite-dimensional unit sphere. It is nonempty, as the
following actual feasible path confirms without a new computation. For
0<=t<q58 let x=q58-t. The singleton word x with multipliers2 and2(1+x) has the
full2-by2 remainder(2-x^2)[[1,-1],[-1,1]], which is PSD. This follows by
substituting L=[[2,-2],[-2(1+x),2(1+x)]] and T=[[1,0],[x,1]] into D+sym(LT).
Join two copies at bridge
1+u(1+x), then two resulting children at bridge2+x. The accepted join has
positive coefficients, fixed right scaling lambda^2 at both levels, and
actual word

    (x,1+u(1+x),x,2+x,x,1+u(1+x),x).

Its base array is Lambdabar; all coefficients are differentiable in t.
Its tangent has p=(-1,-u,-1,-1,-1,-u,-1) and S=-lambda^2, so is nonzero
and belongs to C58. Normalize it to obtain a point of K58.

Equations(7),(26) show -S>0 everywhere on K58. Hence

    m58=min_((p,Y) in K58)(-sum_i p_i)>0, c=m58/2>0.        (33)

The minimum is a mathematical definition, not a solver output or a new
computation. Its positivity follows from compactness: a sequence of unit
cone points with -S->0 would have a unit limit in the equality set(26).

We now prove that all sufficiently small FEASIBLE joint displacements obey

    -S >=c||(p,Y)||_*.                                    (34)

If this failed, choose nonzero feasible displacements (p_k,Y_k) with
t_k=||(p_k,Y_k)||_*->0 and -S_k<c*t_k. Normalize
(p_k,Y_k)=t_k*(p_hat_k,Y_hat_k). A subsequence of the unit normalized pairs
converges to a pair(p_hat,Y_hat) of norm1. Exact flow and all36 active signs
pass to the limit. Homogeneity of(31) gives

    M(hbar+p_k,Lambdabar+Y_k)/t_k
       =W(p_hat_k,Y_hat_k)+t_k*R2(p_hat_k,Y_hat_k)>=0.

By(32), the second term tends to0 in norm. Thus W(p_hat,Y_hat)>=0 on the
FULL R8, so the unit limit lies in K58. The assumed failure of(34) gives
-sum_i p_hat_i<=c=m58/2, contradicting(33). This proves the existence of
a joint radius r58>0 on which(34) holds for every feasible pair.

Finally use the uniform convergence(30) and h->hbar to choose epsilon>0
such that EVERY feasible array above a word with ||h-hbar||_2<epsilon
satisfies ||(h-hbar,Lambda-Lambdabar)||_*<r58. The cube condition can be
ensured by making epsilon smaller, since hbar is in its interior. Equation
(34) is then exactly the asserted capacity bound(3). At the base it holds
with both sides0. Cauchy--Schwarz also gives

    c||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F <=Abar-A(h)
                          =-sum_i(h_i-hbar_i)
                          <=sqrt(7)||h-hbar||_2,

which proves(4).

This normalization uses the JOINT displacement, so it never assumes bounded
physical coefficient difference quotients. Singular PSD faces and zero leading
signs cause no gap: PSD is passed to a limit after the full exact quadratic
error vanishes, and equality of capacity at a unit limiting tangent is
excluded by the explicit kernel calculation. No Hessian coercivity, smooth
certificate selection, or sufficiency of a truncated second-order path is
assumed.

## 9. Audit, allocation, and literature record

The frozen candidate was3dd2fcd7c187b9bd, SHA256
9a6ab7faab39dd06717d82db1bb0a16ad5999355205131195632f2b239f8a19e.
Its embedded exact JSON was read as data and matched the public job05 JSON.
The independent source SHA256 is
920a3c56f22daf3e73a399136178b6bedce31a08e4f79abf4aed2c3820999c1d.
The full source/input/expected-check freeze is freeze.json in this directory.

The sole readback returned code0 with77 passing checks and63 complete basis
readbacks. External process duration including launch/wait was
0.3650967440044042 seconds; its internal duration through result writing was
0.2913039880077122 seconds. These are nested measurements, not additive.
The ledger charged the FULL5 seconds before launch and has no remaining job.
Its conservative main guard1030->1035 is a reservation, not measured time.
Complete stdout, empty stderr, exact_readback_result.json, and job_receipt.json
are preserved. No mathematical evaluation followed this readback. Later code
only read/wrote text, copied data, recorded metadata, or handled literature.

No optimization, numerical eigensolver, rank computation, symbolic optimizer,
benchmark DP, smoke test, retry, installation, consult, native subagent,
fact_submit or formal verification was used. All older ledgers are untouched.

The query-memory and direct-proving workflows were used. The fact search found
the accepted full original identity but no existing local tangent/neighborhood
fact. Matlas and primary-source web searches considered nonlinear conic local
optimality background. The paper by Bruno F. Lourenco, Ellen H. Fukuda and
Masao Fukushima, arXiv:1701.05298, Proposition6.1, was downloaded and its
definitions and proof were read through the web PDF text extractor. The local
pdftotext command was unavailable (exit127, 'pdftotext: command not found');
no PDF package was installed. That paper's result uses a squared-slack
second-order condition. It is not invoked here: Sections3--8 provide the
specific equality calculation and elementary compactness argument directly.
Thus there is no external theorem used as a black-box premise or requiring
external_refs for this draft.

The proposed theorem is local for the FULL ORIGINAL CERTIFICATE cone. It does
not rule out above-threshold words outside its neighborhood, characterize the
unrestricted worst-case minimax value, or establish the original all-n/all-word
target. Formal verification of this complete joint draft remains unallocated.

## Appendix B. Frozen independent exact-readback source

The input required by this source is exactly Appendix A, saved as
frozen_exact_data.json. Its literal mathematical definitions and arithmetic
operations are included for independent review. The source was run once only.

```python
"""One frozen exact readback. No optimizer, author checker, or rank calculation."""
import json
import sys
import time
import traceback
from fractions import Fraction
from pathlib import Path

START = time.perf_counter()
ROOT = Path('/opt/danus/runtime/projects/gd-exact-profile-20260926/research/tangent_neighborhood_20261001')
RESULT = {'status': 'running', 'checks': [], 'basis_readbacks': []}


class Q:
    __slots__ = ('a', 'b')

    def __init__(self, a=0, b=0):
        self.a, self.b = Fraction(a), Fraction(b)

    @staticmethod
    def of(x):
        if isinstance(x, Q):
            return x
        if isinstance(x, list):
            return Q(*x)
        return Q(x)

    def __add__(self, other):
        other = Q.of(other)
        return Q(self.a + other.a, self.b + other.b)

    __radd__ = __add__

    def __neg__(self):
        return Q(-self.a, -self.b)

    def __sub__(self, other):
        return self + (-Q.of(other))

    def __rsub__(self, other):
        return Q.of(other) - self

    def __mul__(self, other):
        other = Q.of(other)
        return Q(self.a * other.a + 2 * self.b * other.b,
                 self.a * other.b + self.b * other.a)

    __rmul__ = __mul__

    def __truediv__(self, other):
        other = Q.of(other)
        den = other.a * other.a - 2 * other.b * other.b
        if den == 0:
            raise ZeroDivisionError('exact zero field divisor')
        return Q((self.a * other.a - 2 * self.b * other.b) / den,
                 (self.b * other.a - self.a * other.b) / den)

    def __pow__(self, exponent):
        result = Q(1)
        for _ in range(exponent):
            result = result * self
        return result

    def __bool__(self):
        return bool(self.a or self.b)

    def __eq__(self, other):
        other = Q.of(other)
        return self.a == other.a and self.b == other.b

    def encoded(self):
        return [str(self.a), str(self.b)]

    def sign(self):
        a, b = self.a, self.b
        if a == 0:
            return (b > 0) - (b < 0)
        if b == 0:
            return (a > 0) - (a < 0)
        if (a > 0) == (b > 0):
            return 1 if a > 0 else -1
        difference = a * a - 2 * b * b
        return ((a > 0) - (a < 0)) * ((difference > 0) - (difference < 0))


def encode(x):
    if isinstance(x, Q):
        return x.encoded()
    if isinstance(x, list):
        return [encode(v) for v in x]
    if isinstance(x, dict):
        return {k: encode(v) for k, v in x.items()}
    return x


def matrix(rows, cols):
    return [[Q() for _ in range(cols)] for _ in range(rows)]


def multiply(A, B):
    C = matrix(len(A), len(B[0]))
    for i, row in enumerate(A):
        for k, value in enumerate(row):
            if value:
                for j, other in enumerate(B[k]):
                    if other:
                        C[i][j] = C[i][j] + value * other
    return C


def laplacian(X):
    A = [[-x for x in row] for row in X]
    for i in range(len(X)):
        A[i][i] = sum(X[i], Q())
    return A


def check(name, condition, detail=None):
    RESULT['checks'].append({'name': name, 'passed': bool(condition), 'detail': encode(detail)})
    if not condition:
        raise AssertionError(name + ': ' + json.dumps(encode(detail)))


def main():
    data = json.loads((ROOT / 'frozen_exact_data.json').read_text())
    # Literal definitions from the mathematical manuscript, not from its checker.
    q = Q(0, 1)
    ell = 1 + q
    h = [q, Q(2), q, 1 + ell, q, Q(2), q]
    capacity = ell ** 3
    table = {
        (0, 1): Q(2), (1, 0): 2 * ell, (1, 3): 2 * ell,
        (2, 1): 2 * ell * q, (2, 3): 2 * ell ** 2 + 2 * ell * q,
        (3, 1): 2 * ell, (3, 2): 2 * ell ** 3, (3, 7): 2 * ell ** 2,
        (4, 3): 2 * ell * q, (4, 5): 2 * ell ** 2, (4, 7): 2 * ell * q,
        (5, 3): 4 * ell, (5, 4): 2 * ell ** 3, (5, 7): 2 * ell ** 3 + 4 * ell,
        (6, 3): 2 * ell * q, (6, 5): 2 * ell ** 3 * q,
        (6, 7): 2 * ell ** 4 + 2 * ell ** 3 * q + 2 * ell * q,
        (7, 3): 2 * ell, (7, 5): 2 * ell ** 3, (7, 6): 2 * ell ** 5,
    }
    pairs = [(i, j) for i in range(8) for j in range(8) if i != j]
    zeros = [ij for ij in pairs if ij not in table]
    base = matrix(8, 8)
    for (i, j), value in table.items():
        base[i][j] = value
    L0 = laplacian(base)
    T0 = [[Q(1) if i == j else (h[j] if j < i else Q()) for j in range(8)] for i in range(8)]
    s = sum(h, Q())
    omega = [-2 * x for x in h] + [2 * s]
    base_flow = [sum((L0[i][j] for i in range(8)), Q()) - omega[j] for j in range(8)]
    LT = multiply(L0, T0)
    D0 = [x * (2 - x) for x in h] + [-s * (s + 2)]
    M0 = [[(LT[i][j] + LT[j][i]) / 2 + (D0[i] if i == j else Q()) for j in range(8)] for i in range(8)]
    check('seven steps and exact base capacity', capacity == 1 + s, h)
    check('all twenty base entries positive', len(table) == 20 and all(v.sign() == 1 for v in table.values()))
    check('base original flow', all(not x for x in base_flow), base_flow)
    check('base full remainder is zero', all(not x for row in M0 for x in row), M0)

    equalities = [Q.of(v) for v in data['equality_prices']]
    check('sixteen equality prices', len(equalities) == 16)
    a, b = equalities[:8], equalities[8:]
    svec_prices = [Q.of(v) for v in data['PSD_svec']]
    check('twenty-eight svec prices', len(svec_prices) == 28)
    Z = matrix(7, 7)
    index = 0
    for j in range(7):
        for i in range(j + 1):
            value = svec_prices[index] if i == j else svec_prices[index] / q
            Z[i][j] = Z[j][i] = value
            index += 1
    stored_Z = [[Q.of(v) for v in row] for row in data['PSD_matrix']]
    check('sqrt2 column-major svec decodes the supplied matrix', Z == stored_Z, Z)
    sigma = {(i, j): Q.of(v) for i, j, v in data['all36_sign_prices']}
    check('all thirty-six original zero-pair prices, no duplicates', len(data['all36_sign_prices']) == 36 and set(sigma) == set(zeros))
    signs = [{'pair': list(ij), 'price': sigma[ij], 'sign': sigma[ij].sign()} for ij in zeros]
    check('all sign prices strictly positive in the real embedding', all(sigma[ij].sign() == 1 for ij in zeros), signs)
    box_prices = [Q.of(v) for v in data['all14_box_prices']]
    check('all fourteen box prices exactly zero', len(box_prices) == 14 and all(not v for v in box_prices), box_prices)

    lower = [[Q.of(v) for v in row] for row in data['LDL']['L']]
    pivots = [Q.of(v) for v in data['LDL']['pivots']]
    check('LDL dimensions', len(lower) == 7 and all(len(row) == 7 for row in lower) and len(pivots) == 7)
    check('LDL unit lower triangular', all(lower[i][i] == 1 for i in range(7)) and all(not lower[i][j] for i in range(7) for j in range(i + 1, 7)))
    check('all seven LDL pivots strictly positive', all(d.sign() == 1 for d in pivots), [{'value': d, 'sign': d.sign()} for d in pivots])
    ldl_residual = [[sum((lower[i][k] * pivots[k] * lower[j][k] for k in range(7)), Q()) - Z[i][j] for j in range(7)] for i in range(7)]
    check('every supplied LDL product entry equals Z', all(not v for row in ldl_residual for v in row), ldl_residual)

    # Independent V Z V^T, where V has columns e_i-e_7.
    H = matrix(8, 8)
    for i in range(7):
        for j in range(7):
            H[i][j] = Z[i][j]
        H[i][7] = -sum(Z[i], Q())
        H[7][i] = H[i][7]
    H[7][7] = sum((sum(row, Q()) for row in Z), Q())
    t0e = [sum(row, Q()) for row in T0]
    labels = [('p', i, -1) for i in range(7)] + [('Y', i, j) for i, j in pairs]
    residuals = []
    for kind, row, col in labels:
        p = [Q() for _ in range(7)]
        Y = matrix(8, 8)
        if kind == 'p':
            p[row] = Q(1)
        else:
            Y[row][col] = Q(1)
        S = sum(p, Q())
        LY = laplacian(Y)
        Tp = [[p[j] if j < i else Q() for j in range(8)] for i in range(8)]
        term1, term2 = multiply(L0, Tp), multiply(LY, T0)
        D1 = [2 * (1 - h[i]) * p[i] for i in range(7)] + [-2 * capacity * S]
        W = [[(term1[i][j] + term2[i][j] + term1[j][i] + term2[j][i]) / 2 + (D1[i] if i == j else Q()) for j in range(8)] for i in range(8)]
        op = [-2 * x for x in p] + [2 * S]
        f = [sum((LY[i][j] for i in range(8)), Q()) - op[j] for j in range(8)]
        n = [sum(W[i], Q()) for i in range(8)]
        B = [[W[i][j] - W[i][7] - W[7][j] + W[7][7] for j in range(7)] for i in range(7)]
        trace = sum((Z[i][j] * B[i][j] for i in range(7) for j in range(7)), Q())
        original_trace = sum((H[i][j] * W[i][j] for i in range(8) for j in range(8)), Q())
        svec_B = [B[i][j] if i == j else q * B[i][j] for j in range(7) for i in range(j + 1)]
        vector_trace = sum((x * y for x, y in zip(svec_prices, svec_B)), Q())
        price_sign_term = sigma.get((row, col), Q()) if kind == 'Y' else Q()
        value = trace + price_sign_term - sum((a[i] * f[i] + b[i] * n[i] for i in range(8)), Q())
        residual = value + S
        label = kind + str(row) + (',' + str(col) if kind == 'Y' else '')
        common_null_residual = sum(n, Q()) - sum((f[i] * t0e[i] for i in range(8)), Q())
        RESULT['basis_readbacks'].append(encode({'variable': label, 'S': S, 'flow': f, 'W': W, 'We': n, 'B': B, 'PSD_price': trace, 'sign_price': price_sign_term, 'stationarity_residual': residual, 'congruence_price_residual': original_trace - trace, 'svec_price_residual': vector_trace - trace, 'common_null_scalar_residual': common_null_residual}))
        check('original-variable coefficient ' + label, not residual and original_trace == trace and vector_trace == trace and not common_null_residual, residual)
        residuals.append(residual)
    RESULT['independently_rebuilt_model'] = encode({'hbar': h, 'capacity': capacity, 'Lambdabar': base, 'T0': T0, 'original_ordered_pairs': [list(ij) for ij in pairs], 'zero_pairs': [list(ij) for ij in zeros], 'Z': Z, 'H': H})
    RESULT['all_63_stationarity_residuals'] = encode(residuals)
    RESULT['stored_passed_flags_used'] = False
    RESULT['stored_stationarity_residuals_used'] = False
    RESULT['author_checker_imported'] = False
    RESULT['status'] = 'passed_exact_readback_not_formal_verification'


try:
    main()
except BaseException as error:
    RESULT['status'] = 'failed_no_retry'
    RESULT['exception'] = repr(error)
    RESULT['traceback'] = traceback.format_exc()
    traceback.print_exc()
finally:
    RESULT['internal_seconds_before_result_write'] = time.perf_counter() - START
    (ROOT / 'exact_readback_result.json').write_text(json.dumps(encode(RESULT), indent=2) + '\n')
    print(json.dumps({'status': RESULT['status'], 'checks': len(RESULT['checks']), 'basis_readbacks': len(RESULT['basis_readbacks']), 'internal_seconds_through_result_write': time.perf_counter() - START}))
sys.exit(0 if RESULT['status'] == 'passed_exact_readback_not_formal_verification' else 1)
```

# Independent exact tangent audit and a full local capacity theorem

Status: COMPLETE JOINT RESEARCH DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. No fact submission or formal
gate was allocated or used. The exact arithmetic readback is an independently
implemented finite check, not verifier acceptance. Every new lemma and the local
theorem below belong to this one joint draft; no earlier unverified finding is
used as an accepted premise. The original all-word exact-profile goal is OPEN.

Worker max, round58, under TASK.md and master_guidance d68a7dd9e0669f07.
The later guidance b5768bac207b0736 explicitly preserves this round and confirms
that its sole exact job is consumed; no new allocation is inferred.
Plan: 24d816a4ff465743. Exactly one mathematical job was run. Its five-second
slot was fully reserved before launch; the public ledger is closed.

Part A verdict: the complete frozen first-order object survives the independent
original-variable exact readback. All63 coefficients, all36 strict sign prices,
all14 zero box prices, the svec convention, and the full dual LDL product pass.

Part B verdict at DRAFT level: the full zero-capacity tangent consists only of
the zero direction. This yields a genuine neighborhood theorem for EVERY full
original certificate, with a strict linear capacity loss and a uniform bound
on coefficient displacement. The proof rederives base uniqueness and local
boundedness jointly and controls the complete nonlinear remainder. Neither a
global seven-step capacity ceiling nor the original minimax problem is settled.

## 1. Objects, accepted input, and the proposed theorem

All vectors and matrices below are real unless exact field data are specified.
Write q58=sqrt(2), lambda=1+q58, u=q58-1=1/lambda, and

    hbar=(q58,2,q58,1+lambda,q58,2,q58),  Abar=lambda^3.

For h=(h0,...,h6) in [0,infinity)^7, let s=sum_i h_i and A(h)=1+s.
Let e be the all-ones column in R8, and let e_i be its coordinate columns,
indexed0,...,7. For any zero-diagonal real8-by8 array X define

    L(X)=diag(Xe)-X.

The prefix matrix T(h) has diagonal1, entry h_j at i,j when j<i, and zero
above its diagonal. Put

    omega(h)=(-2h0,...,-2h6,2s)^T,
    D(h)=diag(h0(2-h0),...,h6(2-h6),-s(s+2)),
    M(h,Lambda)=D(h)+sym(L(Lambda)T(h)),
    sym(K)=(K+K^T)/2.

A full certificate means precisely

    Lambda_ii=0, Lambda_ij>=0 for ALL ordered i!=j,
    L(Lambda)^T e=omega(h), M(h,Lambda)>=0 on the ENTIRE R8.       (1)

All56 ordered coefficients and all8 independent formal gradients remain.
No support, symmetry, rank, or equality between formal gradients is imposed
in(1). Nonzero singular PSD remainders are included.

Accepted fact04219554e41d9750, proof Sections10--11, supplies the original
primitive coefficient identity and the full original-coordinate composition
identity. For a normalized convex1-smooth objective F, write g_i=grad F(x_i)
and f_i=F(x_i); Gram(g0,...,g7) has entries <g_i,g_j>. Its Section10 uses

    x_i=x0-sum_(k<i) h_k g_k,
    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
    V_h=2sum_(i<7)h_i(f_i-f7)
          +sum_(i<7)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g7||^2,

and establishes, under the flow in(1),

    V_h+sum_(i!=j) Lambda_ij Q_ij
       =trace(M(h,Lambda) Gram(g0,...,g7)).                    (2)

This accepted fact is the only non-elementary accepted input used here.
Its original all-function interpretation is retained; no converse about all
primitive schedules is asserted. The exact algebraic statement of its join
identity needed below is recalled in Section5.

Define Lambdabar by the following twenty entries, with every other entry0:

    01:2                    10:2lambda                13:2lambda
    21:2lambda*q58            23:2lambda^2+2lambda*q58    31:2lambda
    32:2lambda^3            37:2lambda^2              43:2lambda*q58
    45:2lambda^2            47:2lambda*q58              53:4lambda
    54:2lambda^3            57:2lambda^3+4lambda      63:2lambda*q58
    65:2lambda^3*q58          67:2lambda^4+2lambda^3*q58+2lambda*q58
    73:2lambda              75:2lambda^3              76:2lambda^5.

These are positive. The independently rebuilt original flow is exact and
M(hbar,Lambdabar)=0. A second algebraic justification is to join two singleton
q58 words at bridge2 and join two resulting three-step words at bridge1+lambda,
using the accepted composition identity; each right scaling is lambda^2 and
each extra interpolation factor is lambda^2-1=2lambda.

PROPOSED LOCAL THEOREM. There exist epsilon>0 and c>0 such that for EVERY
h in[0,infinity)^7 and EVERY array Lambda satisfying(1), if
||h-hbar||_2<epsilon, then

    A(h) <= Abar-c*sqrt(||h-hbar||_2^2+||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F^2). (3)

Here ||.||_F is the Frobenius matrix norm. In particular A(h)<=lambda^3;
equality requires h=hbar and Lambda=Lambdabar. Moreover each such array obeys

    ||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F <= (sqrt(7)/c)*||h-hbar||_2.          (4)

This is a bound relative to the base for all selections, not an assertion of
uniqueness of certificates away from the base or of a Lipschitz selection map
between arbitrary pairs of nearby words. A precise positive definition of c
is given in Section8. The proof establishes existence of epsilon; no numerical
radius is asserted.

## 2. Complete linearization, common null, and exact dual identity

Let E58 be the twenty ordered pairs listed above and Z0 the other36 ordered
distinct pairs. Write Tbal58=T(hbar) and Lbar=L(Lambdabar). Tbal58 is exactly the frozen candidate's prefix matrix T0. The separate name
avoids collision with the benchmark recurrence map called T0 in the glossary.
For p in R7 and a zero-diagonal Y in R^(8-by-8), put

    S=sum_(i=0)^6 p_i,
    Tp_ij=p_j if j<i, and0 otherwise,
    op=(-2p0,...,-2p6,2S)^T,
    D1(p)=diag(2(1-hbar_i)p_i for i<7,-2Abar*S),
    W(p,Y)=D1(p)+sym(Lbar*Tp+L(Y)*Tbal58),
    f(p,Y)=L(Y)^T e-op, n(p,Y)=W(p,Y)e.                     (5)

The FULL linearized cone is

    C58={(p,Y): f(p,Y)=0, Y_ij>=0 on Z0, W(p,Y)>=0 on R8}.    (6)

The twenty Y entries on E58 are free. No p-box occurs in(6).

For any h and any real Lambda with flow, L(Lambda)e=0 and

    omega(h)^T T(h)e=s^2+sum_i h_i^2,
    e^T D(h)e=-s^2-sum_i h_i^2,

so e^T M(h,Lambda)e=0. To check the first equality directly, the entries of
T(h)e are1+sum_(j<i)h_j; expanding the product uses
2sum_(j<i)h_i h_j=s^2-sum_i h_i^2. Differentiation along the exactly flowing
linear families in(5) gives e^T W e=0 when f=0. If W is PSD, expansion of
(e+t z)^T W(e+t z) for arbitrary real t and z forces z^T W e=0, hence n=0.

Let V be the8-by7 matrix whose columns are e_i-e_7 for i<7. Its columns span
e-perp. Put B=V^T W V and G=V^T V=I7+11^T, where1 is the all-ones column
of length7. When We=0, every vector is a multiple of e plus a vector Vz;
therefore W>=0 on R8 if and only if B>=0 on R7. The same decomposition shows
W>=m(I8-ee^T/8) if and only if B>=mG for any real m. Thus the common-null
congruence preserves the original PSD condition without discarding a gradient.

Appendix A contains the complete exact field data for vectors alpha,beta in
Q(q58)^8, a symmetric Z in Q(q58)^(7-by-7), and prices sigma_ij for ij in Z0.
Alpha and beta are respectively the first and last eight equality_prices.
The 28 PSD_svec values are in upper-triangular column-major order. A diagonal
entry is Z_ii; an off-diagonal entry is q58*Z_ij. The precise identity is

    -S = trace(ZB)+sum_(ij in Z0)sigma_ij Y_ij
                  -alpha^T f-beta^T n.                     (7)

It holds for EVERY p and EVERY real zero-diagonal Y, before imposing(6).
All14 box prices are exactly0. Consequently(7) is homogeneous and applies
to arbitrary p; no normalization from the discovery optimization survives.

Here are also the63 coefficient equations in direct algebraic form. Let
H=VZV^T, K=H-sym(beta e^T), let t_i be the transpose of row i of Tbal58, and
let r_ij=t_i-t_j. The unit Y_ij contribution is

    f=e_i-e_j, W=sym(e_i r_ij^T).

Thus the56 equations are

    alpha_i-alpha_j-(K r_ij)_i=0       for ij in E58,
    alpha_i-alpha_j-(K r_ij)_i=sigma_ij for ij in Z0.          (8)

For each i<7 let C_i have entry1 in column i strictly below its diagonal,
and all other entries0. The unit p_i contribution is

    N_i=2(1-hbar_i)e_i e_i^T-2Abar e_7e_7^T+sym(Lbar C_i),
    f=2e_i-2e_7.

The other seven equations are

    2(alpha_i-alpha_7)-trace(K N_i)=1, i=0,...,6.             (9)

The independent executable in Appendix B did NOT use(8)--(9) as a shortcut.
It rebuilt(5) for each of the63 unit inputs from the literal original hbar,
Lambdabar, prefix matrix and row-sum Laplacian. For every input it checked(7),
trace(ZB)=trace(HW), the q58-weighted svec inner product, and

    e^T W e=f^T Tbal58 e.

Its complete result retains all original f,W,We,B arrays. Every coefficient
residual is the rational pair(0,0). Source, input and expected-check hashes
were frozen before the sole charged job. No supplied passed flag or supplied
stationarity residual was used; no author checker was imported.

Field arithmetic uses pairs(r,s) representing r+s*q58, with
(r,s)*(a,b)=(ra+2sb,rb+sa). Inversion multiplies by the conjugate and divides
by r^2-2s^2. Every denominator check is exact. For signs, if r,s have the
same nonzero sign use that sign; if they have opposite signs use
sign(r)*sign(r^2-2s^2). Zero-coordinate cases are immediate. Appendix A's
36 sigma values all have sign+1 by this exact rule.

Appendix A also gives Z=R diag(d0,...,d6) R^T. The independent readback checks
that R is unit lower triangular, every one of the49 product entries is Z's
corresponding entry, and all seven d_i have exact sign+1. Thus Z is positive
definite. No floating eigenvalue is a premise. Let r_k be column k of R, a
vector of length7. Since

    trace(ZB)=sum_k d_k r_k^T B r_k,

this trace is nonnegative for PSD B. If it vanishes, each of the seven
nonnegative quadratic forms vanishes; the PSD argument used for the common
null then makes B annihilate the basis formed by the columns of R. Hence B=0.

For(6), f=n=0, B>=0, and every active Y is nonnegative. Identity(7) proves
S<=0. If S=0, strict positivity of every sigma and positive definiteness of Z
give

    Y_ij=0 for every ij in Z0, B=0, W=0.                    (10)

Up to this point a nonzero equality direction has NOT been excluded. The
next two sections establish its exclusion by original-coordinate algebra.

## 3. A three-step zero-remainder derivative calculation

Use local indices0,1,2,3 and the three-step base word(q58,2,q58), of capacity
lambda^2. In this section L,T,omega,D,M are defined exactly as in Section1
with three steps and four gradients, so the terminal index is3 and e is the
all-ones column of R4. Its base array has seven positive entries

    01=2, 10=13=31=2lambda, 21=2lambda*q58,
    23=2lambda^2+2lambda*q58, 32=2lambda^3,                    (11)

and all other entries0. Its remainder is0 by the accepted singleton join.
Permit derivatives on exactly these seven pairs AND the additional pair03.
The latter derivative is an unrestricted real number. Write r0,r1,r2 for
the physical derivatives, y_ij for the coefficient derivatives, and assume
the four original flow derivatives hold and the full4-by4 remainder
derivative Wloc is0. We prove all r and y are0.

Only the pairs in(11) and03 are used in the following displayed entries.
The entry02 of L(Lambda)T is -h2*Lambda03, whereas its entry20 is0.
Therefore

    (Wloc)_02=-q58*y03/2,

which forces y03=0. The flow at0 and the entries00 and01 now give

    y01-y10=-2r0,
    -2q*r0-u*y01=0,
    -y01+2lambda*r0+u*y10=0.                               (12)

The first two equations give y10=y01+2r0 and y01=-2q*lambda*r0.
Substitution in the last equation yields

    [-2q*lambda*(u-1)+4q]r0=4lambda*r0=0.

Thus r0=y01=y10=0. Entries11 and13 and flow at1 give, in order,

    y13=-2(1+lambda)r1,
    y31=y13-2lambda*r1,
    y21=2(1+lambda)r1.                                     (13)

For clarity, these equations come from

    (Wloc)_11=-2(1+lambda)r1-y13,
    2(Wloc)_13=-y13+y31+2lambda*r1,
    y13-y21-y31=-2r1.

The12 entry, obtained from -h2*Lambda13+(h1-1)*Lambda21, gives

    0=-q58*y13-2lambda*r2+y21+2lambda*q58*r1
      =4lambda^2*r1-2lambda*r2.

Consequently r2=2lambda*r1.

A scalar identity supplies the last physical equation without eliminating
any formal gradient. For a word h of length3 let v=T(h)^(-1)e. Its entries
satisfy v0=1 and v_(i+1)=(1-h_i)v_i. Since L(Lambda)e=0,

    v^T M(h,Lambda)v=v^T D(h)v=1-A(h)^2*v3^2.               (14)

The second equality follows from
h_i(2-h_i)v_i^2=v_i^2-v_(i+1)^2 and -s(s+2)=1-A^2.
At the base, M=0, A=lambda^2 and v3=-u^2. Differentiating(14) therefore
gives the necessary equality

    (r0+r1+r2)/lambda^2+lambda*(r0+r2)+r1=0.               (15)

The moving-vector derivative contributes0 because the base matrix is0.
Substituting r0=0 and r2=2lambda*r1 into(15) gives

    [(1+2lambda)/lambda^2+2lambda^2+1]r1=0.

The bracket is strictly positive, so r1=r2=0. Equations(13) give
y13=y31=y21=0. Entry22 now reads y21-u*y23=0, so y23=0; flow at2 gives
y21+y23-y32=0, so y32=0. Together with(12) and y03=0, this proves that
the full derivative is zero. No sign assumption on these eight derivatives
was needed. This additional03 entry is retained because it arises when the
right child of the seven-step array is extracted.

## 4. Exact central-cut algebra to first order

Assume p,Y satisfy f=0, W=0, and Y=0 on Z0. Thus all coefficient derivatives
are supported on E58. Introduce the REAL, not assumed feasible, families

    h(t)=hbar+t*p, Lambda(t)=Lambdabar+t*Y.

Their flow holds exactly for every real t. Their full remainder is O(t^2),
entry by entry, because its constant and linear coefficients are0. Here
O(t^2) means a function with absolute value bounded by a fixed multiple of
t^2 for sufficiently small |t|; the constants may depend on the fixed p,Y.
All computations in this section retain constant and first-order terms.

Let P58={0,1,2,3}, Bset={4,5,6,7}, and let eP,eB be their indicator columns
in R8. Define capacities and bridge excess from these ACTUAL physical steps:

    a(t)=1+h0+h1+h2, b(t)=1+h4+h5+h6, d(t)=h3-1.

Write h_left=(h0,h1,h2) and h_right=(h4,h5,h6). At t=0, a=b=lambda^2 and
d=lambda. The only P58-to-Bset coefficient is
N(t)=Lambda37. The only Bset-to-P58 coefficients are mu_j(t)=Lambda_(j,3)
for j=4,5,6,7. This support follows from(10) and the displayed E58, rather
than from an ansatz about general feasible certificates.

Let LambdaL be the P58-by-P58 subarray, interpreted as a three-step certificate
array for h0,h1,h2. Its original flow holds exactly: the first three rows
have no crossing edge, and the terminal flow follows by summing all four
row-minus-column sums. Direct matrix multiplication gives

    M(h,Lambda)_(P58,P58)
      =M(h_left,LambdaL)+(a^2-d^2-d*N)diag(0,0,0,1).        (16)

Indeed the only crossing contribution within this principal block is
N*(T_3-T_7) in row3; it changes only entry33, by -d*N. The parent-versus-child
D difference at that entry is (1-d^2)-(1-a^2)=a^2-d^2. The other entries
agree. Thus every derivative entry of M(h_left,LambdaL) except possibly33
is0. The scalar flow identity e^T M e=0 from Section2 also holds for this
child at every t, forcing the remaining derivative entry to vanish. Hence

    M(h_left,LambdaL)=O(t^2), a^2-d^2-d*N=O(t^2).            (17)

For j in Bset, direct multiplication by eP yields

    2(M eP)_j=d*mu_j-N*h_j, j=4,5,6,
    2(M eP)_7=d*mu_7-N.                                  (18)

To see every contribution in(18), T eP equals a+d on Bset and a at index3,
so (L T eP)_j=d*mu_j. On Bset, L^T eP has only the entry -N at7.
Upper triangular multiplication by T^T therefore contributes -N*h_j
for j<7 and -N at7. D eP vanishes on Bset. This proves(18) without
any positivity or remainder-rank hypothesis.

Since M=O(t^2) and d stays positive, (18) gives

    mu_j=(N/d)h_j+O(t^2), j=4,5,6;
    mu_7=N/d+O(t^2); sum_j mu_j=N*b/d+O(t^2).

Summing the exact original flow over P58 gives

    sum_j mu_j-N=2(a+d).

It follows that

    N(b-d)=2d(a+d)+O(t^2).                                (19)

Multiplying(19) by d and using d*N=a^2-d^2+O(t^2) from(17), then dividing
by the positive a+d, gives

    (a-d)(b-d)=2d^2+O(t^2),
    a*b-d(a+b)-d^2=O(t^2).                                (20)

This saturated-bridge relation has been DERIVED for the zero-remainder
derivative; it was not imposed on the full tangent cone.

## 5. Extraction of both complete children and the full equality kernel

For positive a,b near lambda^2 define the positive root

    dhat=(-(a+b)+sqrt((a+b)^2+4ab))/2,
    Cjoin=a+b+dhat, kappa=(Cjoin/b)^2.

These functions are twice continuously differentiable for a,b in a sufficiently
small positive neighborhood of their base values, so all derivatives below exist.

The identity a*b=dhat*Cjoin is exact. Equation(20) and the factorization

    a*b-d(a+b)-d^2=-(d-dhat)(a+b+d+dhat)

show d-dhat=O(t^2). Consequently (17)--(19) yield

    N=dhat*(kappa-1)+O(t^2),
    mu_j=(kappa-1)h_j+O(t^2), j=4,5,6,
    mu_7=kappa-1+O(t^2).                                  (21)

For the first equality use N=(a^2-d^2)/d+O(t^2), replace d by dhat, and
use a/dhat=Cjoin/b. All divisions have nonzero positive base values.

Define a real child array on Bset, with local indices0,...,3, by

    LambdaR_ij=Lambda_(i,j)/kappa
       -(1-1/kappa)h_i*1_(i<7,j=7), i,j in Bset, i!=j.     (22)

Keep its diagonal0. No sign assumption on LambdaR is made. Its nonterminal
flow is -2h_j+O(t^2): the RR subarray's flow at j<7 equals -2h_j-mu_j;
after division and subtraction in(22) it is

    (-2h_j-mu_j)/kappa-(1-1/kappa)h_j=-2h_j+O(t^2).

The terminal flow then follows by summing the four row-minus-column sums.

Here is the exact full-space composition identity used to extract remainders.
For our two physical child words of capacities a,b and bridge1+dhat, the
bridge index is3 and the terminal index is7. Let j range over4,5,6, the right
child's nonterminal indices. Accepted fact04219554e41d9750, Section11, gives

    V_parent=V_left+kappa*V_right-(kappa-1)*Ejoin,
    Ejoin=sum_j h_j Q_(j,3)+Q_(7,3)+dhat Q_(3,7)
                                             +sum_j h_j Q_(j,7). (23)

Its coefficient proof uses dhat*(a+b+dhat)=ab and kappa=((a+b+dhat)/b)^2.
Thus the joined multiplier array consists of LambdaL on P58, kappa*LambdaR
on Bset plus (kappa-1)h_i into7 from each nonterminal right index, and
the cross multipliers dhat(kappa-1), (kappa-1)h_j, kappa-1. Under child flow
the FULL remainder is the disjoint original-coordinate block sum
M_left plus kappa*M_right. This is an algebraic coefficient identity; its
validity does not require the child arrays to be nonnegative or their
remainders to be PSD. In the current application child flow holds through
first order, which is precisely the order at which we use this identity.
More explicitly, for an arbitrary real child array with flow defect
xi=L(Lambda)^T e-omega, collecting the same coefficients as in(2) gives

    V+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=trace(M Gram)
                           +sum_i xi_i(f_i-||g_i||^2/2).

The function-value coefficients gain xi_i and the gradient diagonal gains
-xi_i/2. This exact formula shows explicitly that an O(t^2) flow defect
cannot alter the constant or linear matrix coefficients in the join identity.

Replace the bridge by1+dhat and crossings by the exact expressions in(23).
By(21) these changes are O(t^2); the internal arrays are unchanged. Combining
the original identity(2) with(23), or just collecting its gradient coefficients,
therefore gives

    M(h,Lambda)=M(h_left,LambdaL) direct-sum
                   kappa*M(h_right,LambdaR)+O(t^2).        (24)

The flow errors in(22) are O(t^2), so cannot alter a constant or linear
coefficient in this equality. Equations(17) and(24) imply the right child
remainder is also O(t^2). Thus both children have exact base flow, zero
base remainder, correct derivative flow, and zero derivative remainder.

The left child's derivative support is the seven pairs in(11). The right
child's support is those same seven pairs plus03. Explicitly its original
internal allowed pairs are45,47,54,57,65,67,75,76; the subtractions in(22)
only affect47,57,67. Hence no other pair is introduced. At the base,
kappa=lambda^2 and1-1/kappa=2/lambda. Substitution in(22) gives

    LambdaR_01=2, LambdaR_03=0,
    LambdaR_10=LambdaR_13=LambdaR_31=2lambda,
    LambdaR_21=2lambda*q58,
    LambdaR_23=2lambda^2+2lambda*q58,
    LambdaR_32=2lambda^3.

For example its03 entry is (2lambda*q58)/lambda^2-(2/lambda)q58=0;
its13 entry is (2lambda^3+4lambda)/lambda^2-(2/lambda)*2=2lambda;
its23 entry is
(2lambda^4+2lambda^3*q58+2lambda*q58)/lambda^2-(2/lambda)q58
=2lambda^2+2lambda*q58.
The other five listed entries are obtained by division by lambda^2.

Apply Section3 separately to these two complete four-gradient derivatives.
It gives

    p0=p1=p2=p4=p5=p6=0,
    LambdaL'(0)=LambdaR'(0)=0.

Differentiating(20) now leaves

    -(2lambda^2+2lambda)*p3=0,

so p3=0. Then a'=b'=dhat'=kappa'=0. Equations(21) give N'=mu_j'=0,
and differentiating(22) gives all original RR coefficient derivatives0.
The PP and crossing derivatives are already0. Consequently

    f=0, W=0, Y|Z0=0  ==>  p=0 and Y=0.                   (25)

This conclusion was obtained by textual exact algebra only, with no rank
computation, machine elimination or extra mathematical job. Together with(10)
it identifies the FULL cone's zero-capacity equality set:

    (p,Y) in C58 and S=0  iff  (p,Y)=(0,0).                (26)

## 6. Uniqueness at the base, rederived from the same joint identity

Let Lambda be ANY full feasible array at hbar. Put p=0 and
Y=Lambda-Lambdabar. The full flow gives f=0. Because p=0, no nonlinear term
occurs: W(0,Y)=M(hbar,Lambda). On each of the36 zero base entries,
Y_ij=Lambda_ij>=0. Thus (0,Y) belongs to C58 and has S=0. Equation(26)
gives Y=0. We have proved

    Lambda feasible at hbar ==> Lambda=Lambdabar.          (27)

This proof covers every base certificate and every PSD rank. It does not
assume the all-base uniqueness claim of the earlier b937bb05ba1645b1 draft.

## 7. Uniform bounds and convergence for arbitrary nearby certificate choices

Work inside the compact cube h_i in[1,4], which contains hbar in its interior.
Consider the ONE scalar function F(x)=k*x^2/2, with k=1/8, minimizer0 and
x0=1. Its actual GD positions satisfy

    x_i=product_(j<i)(1-h_j/8), 1/2<=1-h_j/8<=7/8.

They strictly decrease and stay positive. If0<=i<j<=7, then

    x_i-x_j >= x_i-x_(i+1)=(h_i/8)x_i >=2^-9,

because i<=6 and x_i>=2^-6. Direct expansion of all ordered residuals gives

    Q_ij=-k(1-k)(x_i-x_j)^2/2 <=-7/2^25 for i!=j.           (28)

This checks every original ordered finite pair for the same actual quadratic.
Its gradients are g_i=k*x_i. In V_h, all terms h_i(1-h_i)g_i^2 and the
terminal negative square are nonpositive, whereas f_i-f7<=f_i<=k/2.
Since sum_i h_i<=28, we have V_h<=28k=7/2. Applying(2) to any feasible
Lambda, using full PSD and all its nonnegative entries, gives

    (7/2^25)sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij
       <=sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij(-Q_ij)<=V_h<=7/2.

Hence EVERY certificate in this cube satisfies

    sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij<=2^24.                             (29)

The bound is intentionally coarse and purely analytic. In particular the
entire set of coefficient arrays is bounded independently of their selection.

Now take any sequence h_k->hbar and choose any feasible Lambda_k. Eventually
the words lie in the cube. A subsequence of the arrays converges by(29).
The limit is nonnegative with zero diagonal, has the exact base flow, and has
a PSD base remainder by continuity: each quadratic form is a limit of
nonnegative quadratic forms. Equation(27) identifies the limit as Lambdabar.
If uniform convergence over all selections failed, one could choose such a
sequence with ||Lambda_k-Lambdabar||_F bounded away from0; its convergent
subsequence would contradict(27). Therefore

    h->hbar through feasible words implies Lambda->Lambdabar
    uniformly over ALL feasible choices of Lambda.         (30)

No estimate on ||Lambda-Lambdabar||/||h-hbar|| was used. Such quotients are
allowed to be unbounded at this stage of the argument.

## 8. Exact nonlinear remainder and strict local capacity loss

For arbitrary finite displacements p=h-hbar, Y=Lambda-Lambdabar, the full
polynomial expansion is EXACT:

    M(h,Lambda)=W(p,Y)+R2(p,Y),
    R2(p,Y)=diag(-p0^2,...,-p6^2,-S^2)+sym(L(Y)Tp).          (31)

Flow is exactly f(p,Y)=0. Every original sign is retained:
Y_ij>=0 on Z0 and Lambdabar_ij+Y_ij>=0 on E58. There are no omitted terms in
(31). In particular the mixed coefficient-step term is retained, and higher
orders arising from a nonlinear choice of h,Lambda are already covered by
this exact displacement formula.

Put ||(p,Y)||_* = sqrt(||p||_2^2+||Y||_F^2). Cauchy--Schwarz gives

    ||L(Y)||_F <=(sqrt(7)+1)||Y||_F,
    ||Tp||_F <=sqrt(7)||p||_2,
    ||diag(-p_i^2,-S^2)||_F <=||p||_2^2+S^2<=8||p||_2^2.

The first bound uses seven off-diagonal entries in each row. The second uses
||Tp||_F^2=sum_(j=0)^6(7-j)p_j^2. Symmetrization does not increase Frobenius
norm, and the Frobenius norm of a product is bounded by the product of norms.
Consequently

    ||R2(p,Y)||_F <=8||p||_2^2+(7+sqrt(7))||p||_2||Y||_F
                   <=13||(p,Y)||_*^2.                     (32)

Define the compact set

    K58={(p,Y) in C58: ||(p,Y)||_*=1}.

It is closed because flow and W are linear and the sign and PSD cones are
closed; it lies on a finite-dimensional unit sphere. It is nonempty, as the
following actual feasible path confirms without a new computation. For
0<=t<q58 let x=q58-t. The singleton word x with multipliers2 and2(1+x) has the
full2-by2 remainder(2-x^2)[[1,-1],[-1,1]], which is PSD. This follows by
substituting L=[[2,-2],[-2(1+x),2(1+x)]] and T=[[1,0],[x,1]] into D+sym(LT).
Join two copies at bridge
1+u(1+x), then two resulting children at bridge2+x. The accepted join has
positive coefficients, fixed right scaling lambda^2 at both levels, and
actual word

    (x,1+u(1+x),x,2+x,x,1+u(1+x),x).

Its base array is Lambdabar; all coefficients are differentiable in t.
Its tangent has p=(-1,-u,-1,-1,-1,-u,-1) and S=-lambda^2, so is nonzero
and belongs to C58. Normalize it to obtain a point of K58.

Equations(7),(26) show -S>0 everywhere on K58. Hence

    m58=min_((p,Y) in K58)(-sum_i p_i)>0, c=m58/2>0.        (33)

The minimum is a mathematical definition, not a solver output or a new
computation. Its positivity follows from compactness: a sequence of unit
cone points with -S->0 would have a unit limit in the equality set(26).

We now prove that all sufficiently small FEASIBLE joint displacements obey

    -S >=c||(p,Y)||_*.                                    (34)

If this failed, choose nonzero feasible displacements (p_k,Y_k) with
t_k=||(p_k,Y_k)||_*->0 and -S_k<c*t_k. Normalize
(p_k,Y_k)=t_k*(p_hat_k,Y_hat_k). A subsequence of the unit normalized pairs
converges to a pair(p_hat,Y_hat) of norm1. Exact flow and all36 active signs
pass to the limit. Homogeneity of(31) gives

    M(hbar+p_k,Lambdabar+Y_k)/t_k
       =W(p_hat_k,Y_hat_k)+t_k*R2(p_hat_k,Y_hat_k)>=0.

By(32), the second term tends to0 in norm. Thus W(p_hat,Y_hat)>=0 on the
FULL R8, so the unit limit lies in K58. The assumed failure of(34) gives
-sum_i p_hat_i<=c=m58/2, contradicting(33). This proves the existence of
a joint radius r58>0 on which(34) holds for every feasible pair.

Finally use the uniform convergence(30) and h->hbar to choose epsilon>0
such that EVERY feasible array above a word with ||h-hbar||_2<epsilon
satisfies ||(h-hbar,Lambda-Lambdabar)||_*<r58. The cube condition can be
ensured by making epsilon smaller, since hbar is in its interior. Equation
(34) is then exactly the asserted capacity bound(3). At the base it holds
with both sides0. Cauchy--Schwarz also gives

    c||Lambda-Lambdabar||_F <=Abar-A(h)
                          =-sum_i(h_i-hbar_i)
                          <=sqrt(7)||h-hbar||_2,

which proves(4).

This normalization uses the JOINT displacement, so it never assumes bounded
physical coefficient difference quotients. Singular PSD faces and zero leading
signs cause no gap: PSD is passed to a limit after the full exact quadratic
error vanishes, and equality of capacity at a unit limiting tangent is
excluded by the explicit kernel calculation. No Hessian coercivity, smooth
certificate selection, or sufficiency of a truncated second-order path is
assumed.

# Explicit alternative-state tests for the three terminal screens

Status: JOINT UNVERIFIED. These tests refute weaker universal claims only.
They do not assert that any tested state is reached from e_1.
The frozen static function, backward definitions, physical word and true
radius are exactly those of joint_analysis.md, shared finding8dfaa8cef518215c.
No new function, step, computation or formal submission is introduced.

Use its C=beta+sqrt(1+2beta), kappa=1+beta^2, sigma_i, ell_i, A_i,D_i.
A component vanishes exactly when x_j<=A_j and
x_(j+1)>=beta*(x_j-ell_j).

## A. Cross-time pairing need not be nonnegative

Choose 2<=i<=k-2 and a physical suffix step h=b_(m-2) satisfying

    h>2kappa+2ell_(i+2)/(beta*sigma_i)
                                 +2C*sigma_(i+1)/sigma_i.
    alpha=h/2.

Set x_i=A_i, x_(i+1)=ell_(i+1)-kappa*sigma_i,
x_(i+2)=ell_(i+2), and x_j=0 for i+3<=j<=k.
Set x_(k+1)=ell_(k+1)+D_(k+1).

Here is an explicit choice of every earlier coordinate, removing any
implicit "sufficiently negative" selection. The known proposed update
of coordinate i is x_i^+=A_i-alpha*beta*sigma_i. Set

    x_(i-1)=min(A_(i-1),
              ell_(i-1)+min(x_i,x_i^+)/beta)-sigma_(i-1),

and successively for j=i-2,...,1 set

    x_j=min(A_j,ell_j+x_(j+1)/beta)-sigma_j.

Finally replace x_1 by min(x_1,A_1-beta*D_2).
Lowering x_1 cannot spoil its zero-cone inequality. At both the initial
and proposed next samples every component j<i has nonpositive input and
edge residuals. Hence its projection is zero, and (6) leaves all these earlier
coordinates unchanged, verifying the proposed update consistently.

Initially r_i=0,z_i=kappa*sigma_i, so

    (P_i,Q_i)=(beta*sigma_i,sigma_i).

The next component has input <ell_(i+1) and output ell_(i+2), so it is
zero. At j=i+2 the input is ell_(i+2) with nonnegative output, hence zero;
all later retired components are also zero. Therefore the retired
combined-gradient vector before the step is exactly
(beta*sigma_i,-sigma_i) on coordinates i,i+1.

After the step,

    r_i^+=-alpha*beta*sigma_i,
    z_i^+=kappa*sigma_i*(1-alpha)<0,
    r_(i+1)^+=(alpha-kappa)*sigma_i-ell_(i+2)/beta
                                               >C*sigma_(i+1).

Thus component i vanishes, while i+1 is in its horizontal cap since
its output remains ell_(i+2). All earlier components are zero by the
explicit selection; later retired components stay zero. The terminal
scalar remains isolated: its excess after the step is
D_(k+1)*(1-h/[2(1+S_(m-1))])>0, because
S_(m-1)>=b_(m-2)=h. Thus no omitted last gradient enters the retired vector.

The retired combined-gradient vector after the step is exactly
(0,C*sigma_(i+1)) on these coordinates. Consequently

    <d_after,d_before>=-C*sigma_i*sigma_(i+1)<0.

This uses the full static function and its physical available h.
It refutes universal nonnegativity of cross-time retired gradient
pairings. It does not refute an integrated inequality on the actual
initialized trajectory.

## B. Own-checkpoint margins are not sufficient for retirement invariance

Choose 2<=i<=k-1 and h=b_(J+i-2), alpha=h/2. This step occurs in the
complete H_(J+i-1) after checkpoint i. Assume the explicit finite conditions

    beta*D_(i+1)-beta^2*D_i-ell_(i+1)/beta
                                             >=kappa*sigma_(i-1),
    beta*T_i/M_i<1, h*C<2M_(i+1),
    u_(i+1)>=C+sqrt(1+2beta)*y_(i+1)/beta.                 (B1)

Set

    x_(i-1)=A_(i-1)-beta*D_i,
    x_i=A_i-beta*D_(i+1),
    x_(i+1)=ell_(i+1)+D_(i+1),
    x_j=0 for j>=i+2.

If i>=3, choose the earlier coordinates by the same zero-cone recipe
as in A, using boundary coordinate x_(i-1) and its proposed next value

    x_(i-1)^+=x_(i-1)-alpha*beta*sigma_(i-1).

That makes every component j<i-1 zero before and after. Enforce also
x_1<=A_1-beta*D_2 as in A. For i=2 no additional earlier coordinate
is needed: x_1=A_1-beta*D_2 already has the permanent first margin.

Now r_(i-1)=-beta*D_i<0 and

    z_(i-1)=beta*D_(i+1)-beta^2*D_i-ell_(i+1)/beta.

Condition(B1) and the exact edge formula give

    P_(i-1)=beta*sigma_(i-1),
    Q_(i-1)=sigma_(i-1)>0.

The own component i has its exact negative checkpoint margins

    r_i=-beta*D_(i+1), z_i=-kappa*D_(i+1),

so P_i=Q_i=0. The next component is precisely at its local block's
starting pair (ell_(i+1)+D_(i+1),0). The last condition in (B1)
therefore gives P_(i+1)=C*sigma_(i+1), Q_(i+1)=0.
Every old and future derivative is evaluated from the same function;
none is removed in this update.

Using D_i=sigma_(i-1)*(h/2-1),

    r_i^+=-beta*D_(i+1)+alpha*sigma_(i-1)
          =-beta*D_(i+1)+D_i+sigma_(i-1)>0,
    x_(i+1)^+=ell_(i+1)
                         +D_(i+1)*(1-h*C/[2M_(i+1)])
                                                >ell_(i+1).

Its new actual envelope value is consequently the positive horizontal
Huber Hub_(C*sigma_i)(r_i^+). The earlier zero-cone recipe verifies
the proposed previous-coordinate update; for i=2 it follows from Q_0=0.

The conditions in (B1) hold eventually in the joint scale derivation:
the amplitude ratios are uniformly positive, beta=1/k, the sigma and
translation terms are exponentially smaller than beta*D_i, and
h*C/(2M_(i+1)) tends to (lambda-2)/lambda<1.
No numerical threshold or finite-depth m>=1536 claim is inferred.

This refutes invariance based only on own-checkpoint margins and earlier
negative margins. It retains a saturated incoming Q_(i-1) and a legal
later step. The simultaneous tested point is an alternative state; the
true later state must instead be derived from the complete ordered history
in equations(32),(33),(33a). Initialized retirement is neither disproved
nor established.

# Initialized terminal cancellation in the frozen circular chain

JOINT UNVERIFIED ANALYTICAL PROOF ATTEMPT AND SCOPED FAILURE REPORT.
No formal gate was authorized or called. No assertion in this report has
been promoted to a fact. The frozen policy and the original minimax target
remain OPEN.

Project gd-exact-profile-20260926; worker max3; one round after baseline62,
ended ceiling63 / attempted ceiling64. TASK.md, guidance ca0fd834c90cd90a,
and elaboration f5f79a115a09ff55 govern. Plan1083226e9f46fb59 specifies the
three screens. All prior computational and formal allocations remain closed.

The full public evidence2df3df199e391be9, bfed68e4c77df24e,
d5345c6e59f22ce3, and84a4435a88a9f487 was read. Their mathematical assertions
are not accepted premises. The geometry, scales, endpoint estimates and
identities used here are derived jointly below. The sole accepted input is
the original benchmark/ray conclusion of fact04219554e41d9750.

There is a new cap-payment estimate in Section7: during an actual persistent
positive horizontal excursion, a weighted downstream deficit absorbs the
circular cap loss, with a uniform positive loss rate and both neighbors
retained. Its exact entry costs remain unpaid. Sections5 and6 supply,
respectively, an exact terminal-gradient identity retaining cancellation and
the complete initialized incoming interface for every interior component.
None of the three screens gives a uniform bound on the actual middle
terminal value below 1-T_infty. The failures are failures of the stated
comparisons, not a disproof of terminal absorption on the actual trajectory.

## 1. Exact static function, body and physical word

For each integer m>=16 put

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), k=floor(sqrt(m)), J=m-k, beta=1/k,
    eta_word=1-1/(4m), c=lambda*(1+1/(4m)), a_word=eta_word/c,
    d=c-2,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,b_j,H_j), b_j=1+a_word*c^j,
    S_j=sum H_j, n=2^m-1.

Every child uses this SAME enclosing m and these same parameters. Select
the first occurrences of levels J,...,m-1. Checkpoint i has level
l_i=J+i-1, physical step index 2^l_i-1 and value b_l_i.
The ordinary block masses are s_1=S_J, s_i=S_(J+i-2) for 2<=i<=k,
and the last mass is S=S_(m-1). Every entry of the word is executed.

For 0<=q<=1 define

    p(q)=beta+sqrt((1+beta)^2-(q+beta)^2),
    C=beta+sqrt(1+2beta), e=C-beta=sqrt(1+2beta),
    K=conv({0} union {(p(q),-q):0<=q<=1}),
    kappa=1+beta^2.

The complete horizontal sections of this full body are

    0<=q<=1, beta*q<=p<=p(q).                              (1)

It has the two radial segments to (C,0) and (beta,-1), and the whole
circular arc between them. No finite-plane replacement is made.

For a compact convex body B containing0 put

    E_B(w)=max_(g in B){<g,w>-||g||^2/2}.

Write Hub_L for the one-sided Huber: zero for r<=0, r^2/2 for
0<=r<=L, and L*r-L^2/2 for r>=L.

The local data of every ordinary block are defined using its actual order.
If its physical entries are a_1,...,a_N, set alpha_j=a_j/2.
Start (u_N,-y_N)=(beta,-1), q_N=1, and define backwards
